Each lane keeps\n// its own accumulator and iterates over all j in the original order, so results\n// are bit-identical to the sequential version while allowing SIMD over i.\nconstexpr int VLEN = 8;\n\nstruct Vec3 {\n    double x, y, z;\n    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}\n};\n\nstruct Body {\n    Vec3 pos;\n    Vec3 vel;\n};\n\n// Structure-of-arrays state. Positions are replicated on every rank, velocities\n// are only kept for the locally owned block of bodies.\nstruct State {\n    std::vector<double> px, py, pz; // size n, replicated\n    std::vector<double> vx, vy, vz; // size localCount\n};\n\nvoid randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    for (auto& body : bodies) {\n        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n    }\n}\n\n// Computes the forces acting on the locally owned bodies [begin, end) and\n// updates the local velocities. All positions must be up to date.\nvoid computeForces(State& s, const size_t n, const size_t begin, const size_t end) {\n    const double* __restrict px = s.px.data();\n    const double* __restrict py = s.py.data();\n    const double* __restrict pz = s.pz.data();\n    double* __restrict vx = s.vx.data();\n    double* __restrict vy = s.vy.data();\n    double* __restrict vz = s.vz.data();\n\n    size_t i = begin;\n    for (; i + VLEN <= end; i += VLEN) {\n        double xi[VLEN], yi[VLEN], zi[VLEN];\n        double Fx[VLEN] = {}, Fy[VLEN] = {}, Fz[VLEN] = {};\n        for (int l = 0; l < VLEN; ++l) {\n            xi[l] = px[i + l];\n            yi[l] = py[i + l];\n            zi[l] = pz[i + l];\n        }\n\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double xj = px[j], yj = py[j], zj = pz[j];\n            for (int l = 0; l < VLEN; ++l) {\n                const double dx = xj - xi[l];\n                const double dy = yj - yi[l];\n                const double dz = zj - zi[l];\n                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n                Fx[l] += dx * invDist3;\n                Fy[l] += dy * invDist3;\n                Fz[l] += dz * invDist3;\n            }\n        }\n\n        for (int l = 0; l < VLEN; ++l) {\n            vx[i - begin + l] += DT * Fx[l];\n            vy[i - begin + l] += DT * Fy[l];\n            vz[i - begin + l] += DT * Fz[l];\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Remainder\n    for (; i < end; ++i) {\n        double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n        const double xi = px[i], yi = py[i], zi = pz[i];\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double dx = px[j] - xi;\n            const double dy = py[j] - yi;\n            const double dz = pz[j] - zi;\n            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);\n            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n            Fx += dx * invDist3;\n            Fy += dy * invDist3;\n            Fz += dz * invDist3;\n        }\n        vx[i - begin] += DT * Fx;\n        vy[i - begin] += DT * Fy;\n        vz[i - begin] += DT * Fz;\n    }\n}\n\nvoid integrateBodies(State& s, const size_t begin, const size_t end) {\n    for (size_t i = begin; i < end; ++i) {\n        s.px[i] += s.vx[i - begin] * DT;\n        s.py[i] += s.vy[i - begin] * DT;\n        s.pz[i] += s.vz[i - begin] * DT;\n    }\n}\n\n// Distributed total energy. Rows of the potential-energy triangle are assigned\n// round-robin to balance the triangular work; partial sums are combined on rank\n// 0 in rank order so the result is deterministic for a given rank count.\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const State& s, const size_t n, const size_t begin, const size_t end, const int rank,\n    const int size) {\n    double energy = 0.0;\n\n    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass) over the locally owned bodies\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < end - begin; ++i) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (s.vx[i] * s.vx[i] + s.vy[i] * s.vy[i] + s.vz[i] * s.vz[i]);\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)\n    const double* __restrict px = s.px.data();\n    const double* __restrict py = s.py.data();\n    const double* __restrict pz = s.pz.data();\n    for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(rank); i < n; i += static_cast<size_t>(size)) {\n        double partial = 0.0;\n        const double xi = px[i], yi = py[i], zi = pz[i];\n        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double dx = px[j] - xi;\n            const double dy = py[j] - yi;\n            const double dz = pz[j] - zi;\n            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n            partial -= 1.0 / dist;\n        }\n        energy += partial;\n    }\n\n    std::vector<double> partials(rank == 0 ? size : 0);\n    MPI_Gather(&energy, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, partials.data(), 1, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    double total = 0.0;\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        for (const double p : partials) total += p;\n    }\n    return total;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        // Check for NaN or Inf values\n        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||\n            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)\n        const double maxPos = 1e6;\n        const double maxVel = 1e6;\n        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n\n    int Each lane keeps\n// its own accumulator and iterates over all j in the original order, so results\n// are bit-identical to the sequential version while allowing SIMD over i.\nconstexpr int VLEN = 8;\n\nstruct Vec3 {\n    double x, y, z;\n    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}\n};\n\nstruct Body {\n    Vec3 pos;\n    Vec3 vel;\n};\n\n// Structure-of-arrays state. Positions are replicated on every rank, velocities\n// are only kept for the locally owned block of bodies.\nstruct State {\n    std::vector<double> px, py, pz; // size n, replicated\n    std::vector<double> vx, vy, vz; // size localCount\n};\n\nvoid randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    for (auto& body : bodies) {\n        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n    }\n}\n\n// Computes the forces acting on the locally owned bodies [begin, end) and\n// updates the local velocities. All positions must be up to date.\nvoid computeForces(State& s, const size_t n, const size_t begin, const size_t end) {\n    const double* __restrict px = s.px.data();\n    const double* __restrict py = s.py.data();\n    const double* __restrict pz = s.pz.data();\n    double* __restrict vx = s.vx.data();\n    double* __restrict vy = s.vy.data();\n    double* __restrict vz = s.vz.data();\n\n    size_t i = begin;\n    for (; i + VLEN <= end; i += VLEN) {\n        double xi[VLEN], yi[VLEN], zi[VLEN];\n        double Fx[VLEN] = {}, Fy[VLEN] = {}, Fz[VLEN] = {};\n        for (int l = 0; l < VLEN; ++l) {\n            xi[l] = px[i + l];\n            yi[l] = py[i + l];\n            zi[l] = pz[i + l];\n        }\n\n        for (size_t j = 0; l < VLEN; ++l) {\n            vx[i - begin + l] += DT * Fx[l];\n            vy[i - begin + l] += DT * Fy[l];\n            vz[i - begin + l] += DT * Fz[l];\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Remainder\n    for (; i < end; ++i) {\n        double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n        const double xi = px[i], yi = py[i], zi = pz[i];\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double dx = px[j] - xi;\n            const double dy = py[j] - yi;\n            const double dz = pz[j] - zi;\n            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);\n            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n            Fx += dx * invDist3;\n            Fy += dy * invDist3;\n            Fz += dz * invDist3;\n        }\n        vx[i - begin] += DT * Fx;\n        vy[i - begin] += DT * Fy;\n        vz[i - begin] += DT * Fz;\n    }\n}\n\nvoid integrateBodies(State& s, const size_t begin, const size_t end) {\n    for (size_t i = begin; Each lane keeps\n// its own accumulator and iterates over all j in the original order, so results\n// are bit-identical to the sequential version while allowing SIMD over i.\nconstexpr int VLEN = 8;\n\nstruct Vec3 {\n    double x, y, z;\n    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}\n};\n\nstruct Body {\n    Vec3 pos;\n    Vec3 vel;\n};\n\n// Structure-of-arrays state. Positions are replicated on every rank, velocities\n// are only kept for the locally owned block of bodies.\nstruct State {\n    std::vector<double> px, py, pz; // size n, replicated\n    std::vector<double> vx, vy, vz; // size localCount\n};\n\nvoid randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    for (auto& body : bodies) {\n        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n    }\n}\n\n// Computes the forces acting on the locally owned bodies [begin, end) and\n// updates the local velocities. All positions must be up to date.\nvoid computeForces(State& s, const size_t n, const size_t begin, const size_t end) {\n    const double* __restrict px = s.px.data();\n    const double* __restrict py = s.py.data();\n    const double* __restrict pz = s.pz.data();\n    double* __restrict vx = s.vx.data();\n    double* __restrict vy = s.vy.data();\n    double* __restrict vz = s.vz.data();\n\n    size_t i = begin;\n    for (; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double xj = px[j], yj = py[j], zj = pz[j];\n            for (int l = 0; l < VLEN; ++l) {\n                const double dx = xj - xi[l];\n                const double dy = yj - yi[l];\n                const double dz = zj - zi[l];\n                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n                Fx[l] += dx * invDist3;\n                Fy[l] += dy * invDist3;\n                Fz[l] += dz * invDist3;\n            }\n        }\n\n        for (int l = 0; l < VLEN; ++l) {\n            vx[i - begin + l] += DT * Fx[l];\n            vy[i - begin + l] += DT * Fy[l];\n            vz[i - begin + l] += DT * Fz[l];\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Remainder\n    for (; i < end; ++i) {\n        double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n        const double xi = px[i], yi = py[i], zi = pz[i];\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double dx = px[j] - xi;\n            const double dy = py[j] - yi;\n            const double dz = pz[j] - zi;\n            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);\n            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n            Fx += dx * invDist3;\n            Fy += dy * invDist3;\n            Fz += dz * invDist3;\n        }\n        vx[i - begin] += DT * Fx;\n        vy[i - begin] += DT * Fy;\n        vz[i - begin] += DT * Fz;\n    }\n}\n\nvoid integrateBodies(State& s, const size_t begin, const size_t end) {\n    for (size_t i = begin; i < end; ++i) {\n        s.px[i] += s.vx[i - begin] * DT;\n        s.py[i] += s.vy[i - begin] * DT;\n        s.pz[i] += s.vz[i - begin] * DT;\n    }\n}\n\n// Distributed total energy. Rows of the potential-energy triangle are assigned\n// round-robin to balance the triangular work; partial sums are combined on rank\n// 0 in rank order so the result is deterministic for a given rank count.\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const State& s, const size_t n, const size_t begin, const size_t end, const int rank,\n    const int size) {\n    double energy = 0.0;\n\n    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass) over the locally owned bodies\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < end - begin; ++i) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (s.vx[i] * s.vx[i] + s.vy[i] * s.vy[i] + s.vz[i] * s.vz[i]);\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)\n    const double* __restrict px = s.px.data();\n    const double* __restrict py = s.py.data();\n    const double* __restrict pz = s.pz.data();\n    for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(rank); i < n; i += static_cast<size_t>(size)) {\n        double partial = 0.0;\n        const double xi = px[i], yi = py[i], zi = pz[i];\n        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double dx = px[j] - xi;\n            const double dy = py[j] - yi;\n            const double dz = pz[j] - zi;\n            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n            partial -= 1.0 / dist;\n        }\n        energy += partial;\n    }\n\n    std::vector<double> partials(rank == 0 ? size : 0);\n Positions are replicated on every rank, velocities\n// are only kept for the locally owned block of bodies.\nstruct State {\n    std::vector<double> px, py, pz; // size n, replicated\n    std::vector<double> vx, vy, vz; // size localCount\n};\n\nvoid randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    for (auto& body : bodies) {\n        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n    }\n}\n\n// Computes the forces acting on the locally owned bodies [begin, end) and\n// updates the local velocities. Each lane keeps\n// its own accumulator and iterates over all j in the original order, so results\n// are bit-identical to the sequential version while allowing SIMD over i.\nconstexpr int VLEN = 8;\n\nstruct Vec3 {\n    double x, y, z;\n    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}\n};\n\nstruct Body {\n    Vec3 pos;\n    Vec3 vel;\n};\n\n// Structure-of-arrays state. Positions are replicated on every rank, velocities\n// are only kept for the locally owned block of bodies.\nstruct State {\n    std::vector<double> px, py, pz; // size n, replicated\n    std::vector<double> vx, vy, vz; // size localCount\n};\n\nvoid randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    for (auto& body : bodies) {\n        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n    }\n}\n\n// Computes the forces acting on the locally owned bodies [begin, end) and\n// updates the local velocities. All positions must be up to date.\nvoid computeForces(State& s, const size_t n, const size_t begin, const size_t end) {\n    const double* __restrict px = s.px.data();\n    const double* __restrict py = s.py.data();\n    const double* __restrict pz = s.pz.data();\n    double* __restrict vx = s.vx.data();\n    double* __restrict vy = s.vy.data();\n    double* __restrict vz = s.vz.data();\n\n    // The position arrays are padded by VLEN entries, so the last (partial) block\n    // can be processed with the exact same kernel; surplus lanes are discarded.\n    for (size_t i = begin; i < end; i += VLEN) {\n        double xi[VLEN], yi[VLEN], zi[VLEN];\n        double Fx[VLEN] = {}, Fy[VLEN] = {}, Fz[VLEN] = {};\n        for (int l = 0; l < VLEN; ++l) {\n            xi[l] = px[i + l];\n            yi[l] = py[i + l];\n            zi[l] = pz[i + l];\n        }\n\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double xj = px[j], yj = py[j], zj = pz[j];\n            for (int l = 0; l < VLEN; ++l) {\n                const double dx = xj - xi[l];\n                const double dy = yj - yi[l];\n                const double dz = zj - zi[l];\n                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n                Fx[l] += dx * invDist3;\n                Fy[l] += dy * invDist3;\n                Fz[l] += dz * invDist3;\n            }\n        }\n\n        const size_t lanes = end - i < VLEN ? Rows of the potential-energy triangle are assigned\n// round-robin to balance the triangular work; partial sums are combined on rank\n// 0 in rank order so the result is deterministic for a given rank (assuming unit mass) over the locally owned bodies\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < end - begin; ++i) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (s.vx[i] * s.vx[i] + s.vy[i] * s.vy[i] + s.vz[i] * s.vz[i]);\n    }\n\n std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n            partial -= 1.0 / dist;\n        }\n        energy += partial;\n    }\n\n    std::vector<double> partials(rank == 0 ? size : 0);\n return total;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        // Check Each lane keeps\n// its own accumulator and iterates over all j in the original order, so results\n// are bit-identical to the sequential version while allowing SIMD over i.\nconstexpr int VLEN = 8;\n\nstruct Vec3 {\n    double x, y, z;\n    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}\n};\n\nstruct Body {\n    Vec3 pos;\n    Vec3 vel;\n};\n\n// Structure-of-arrays state. Positions are replicated on every rank, velocities\n// are only kept for the locally owned block of bodies.\nstruct State {\n    std::vector<double> px, py, pz; // size n, replicated\n    std::vector<double> vx, vy, vz; // size localCount\n};\n\nvoid randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    for (auto& body : bodies) {\n        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n    }\n}\n\n// Computes the forces acting on the locally owned bodies [begin, end) and\n// updates the local velocities. All positions must be up to date.\nvoid computeForces(State& s, const size_t n, const size_t begin, const size_t end) {\n    const double* __restrict px = s.px.data();\n    const double* __restrict py = s.py.data();\n    const double* __restrict pz = s.pz.data();\n    double* __restrict vx = s.vx.data();\n    double* __restrict vy = s.vy.data();\n    double* __restrict vz = s.vz.data();\n\n    // The position arrays are padded by VLEN entries, so the last (partial) block\n    // can be processed with the exact same kernel; surplus lanes are discarded.\n    for (size_t i = begin; i < end; i += VLEN) {\n        double xi[VLEN], yi[VLEN], zi[VLEN];\n        double Fx[VLEN] = {}, Fy[VLEN] = {}, Fz[VLEN] = {};\n        for (int l = 0; l < VLEN; ++l) {\n            xi[l] = px[i + l];\n            yi[l] = py[i + l];\n            zi[l] = pz[i + l];\n        }\n\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double xj = px[j], yj = py[j], zj = pz[j];\n            for (int l = 0; l < VLEN; ++l) {\n                const double dx = xj - xi[l];\n                const double dy = yj - yi[l];\n                const double dz = zj - zi[l];\n                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n                Fx[l] += dx * invDist3;\n                Fy[l] += dy * invDist3;\n                Fz[l] += dz * invDist3;\n            }\n        }\n\n        const size_t lanes = end - i < VLEN ? end - i : VLEN;\n        for (size_t l = 0; l < lanes; ++l) {\n            vx[i - begin + l] += DT * Fx[l];\n            vy[i - begin + l] += DT * Fy[l];\n            vz[i - begin + l] += DT * Fz[l];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nvoid integrateBodies(State& s, const size_t begin, const size_t end) {\n    for (size_t i = begin; i < end; ++i) {\n        s.px[i] += s.vx[i - begin] * DT;\n        s.py[i] += s.vy[i - begin] * DT;\n        s.pz[i] += s.vz[i - begin] * DT;\n    }\n}\n\n// Distributed total energy. Rows of the potential-energy triangle are assigned\n// round-robin to balance the triangular work; partial sums are combined on rank\n// 0 in rank order so the result is deterministic for a given rank count.\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const State& s, const size_t n, const size_t begin, const size_t end, const int rank,\n    const int size) {\n    double energy = 0.0;\n\n    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass) over the locally owned bodies\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < end - begin; ++i) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (s.vx[i] * s.vx[i] + s.vy[i] * s.vy[i] + s.vz[i] * s.vz[i]);\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)\n    const double* __restrict px = s.px.data();\n    const double* __restrict py = s.py.data();\n    const double* __restrict pz = s.pz.data();\n    for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(rank); i < n; i += static_cast<size_t>(size)) {\n        double partial = 0.0;\n        const double xi = px[i], yi = py[i], zi = pz[i];\n        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double dx = px[j] - xi;\n            const double dy = py[j] - yi;\n            const double dz = pz[j] - zi;\n            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n            partial -= 1.0 / dist;\n        }\n        energy += partial;\n    }\n\n    std::vector<double> partials(rank == 0 ? size : 0);\n    MPI_Gather(&energy, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, partials.data(), 1, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    double total = 0.0;\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        for (const double p : partials) total += p;\n    }\n    return total;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        // Check for NaN or Inf values\n        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||\n            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || Each lane keeps\n// its own accumulator and iterates over all j in the original order, so results\n// are bit-identical to the sequential version while allowing SIMD over i.\nconstexpr int VLEN = 8;\n\nstruct Vec3 {\n    double x, y, z;\n    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}\n};\n\nstruct Body {\n    Vec3 pos;\n    Vec3 vel;\n};\n\n// Structure-of-arrays state. Positions are replicated on every rank, velocities\n// are only kept for the locally owned block of bodies.\nstruct State {\n    std::vector<double> px, py, pz; // size n, replicated\n    std::vector<double> vx, vy, vz; // size localCount\n};\n\nvoid randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    for (auto& body : bodies) {\n        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n    }\n}\n\n// Computes the forces acting on the locally owned bodies [begin, end) and\n// updates the local velocities. All positions must be up to date.\nvoid computeForces(State& s, const size_t n, const size_t begin, const size_t end) {\n    const double* __restrict px = s.px.data();\n    const double* __restrict py = s.py.data();\n    const double* __restrict pz = s.pz.data();\n    double* __restrict vx = s.vx.data();\n    double* __restrict vy = s.vy.data();\n    double* __restrict vz = s.vz.data();\n\n    // The position arrays are padded by VLEN entries, so the last (partial) block\n    // can be processed with the exact same kernel; surplus lanes are discarded.\n    for (size_t i = begin; i < end; i += VLEN) {\n        double xi[VLEN], yi[VLEN], zi[VLEN];\n        double Fx[VLEN] = {}, Fy[VLEN] = {}, Fz[VLEN] = {};\n        for (int l = 0; l < VLEN; ++l) {\n            xi[l] = px[i + l];\n            yi[l] = py[i + l];\n            zi[l] = pz[i + l];\n        }\n\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double xj = px[j], yj = py[j], zj = pz[j];\n            for (int l = 0; l < VLEN; ++l) {\n                const double dx = xj - xi[l];\n                const double dy = yj - yi[l];\n                const double dz = zj - zi[l];\n                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n                Fx[l] += dx * invDist3;\n                Fy[l] += dy * invDist3;\n                Fz[l] += dz * invDist3;\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Velocities are padded as well, so surplus lanes can be stored blindly\n        for (int l = 0; l < VLEN; ++l) {\n            vx[i - begin + l] += DT * Fx[l];\n            vy[i - begin + l] += DT * Fy[l];\n            vz[i - begin + l] += DT * Fz[l];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nvoid integrateBodies(State& s, const size_t begin, const size_t end) {\n    for (size_t i = begin; i < end; ++i) {\n        s.px[i] += s.vx[i - begin] * DT;\n        s.py[i] += s.vy[i - begin] * DT;\n        s.pz[i] += s.vz[i - begin] * DT;\n    }\n}\n\n// Distributed total energy. Rows of the potential-energy triangle are assigned\n// round-robin to balance the triangular work; partial sums are combined on rank\n// 0 in rank order so the result is deterministic for a given rank count.\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const State& s, const size_t n, const size_t begin, const size_t end, const int rank,\n    const int size) {\n    double energy = 0.0;\n\n    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass) over the locally owned bodies\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < end - begin; ++i) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (s.vx[i] * s.vx[i] + s.vy[i] * s.vy[i] + s.vz[i] * s.vz[i]);\n    }\n\n s.pz.data();\n    for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(rank); i < n; i += static_cast<size_t>(size)) {\n        double partial = 0.0;\n        const double xi = px[i], yi = py[i], zi = pz[i];\n std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n            partial -= 1.0 / dist;\n        }\n        energy += partial;\n    }\n\n    std::vector<double> partials(rank == 0 ? size : 0);\n The nbody benchmark now runs unconditionally under MPI.\n\n**Design** (`nbody/nbody.cpp`):\n- **Decomposition**: contiguous block distribution of bodies over ranks. The nbody benchmark now runs unconditionally under MPI.\n\n**Design** (`nbody/nbody.cpp`):\n- **Decomposition**: contiguous block distribution of bodies over ranks. Positions are replicated on every rank (needed by the all-pairs kernel); velocities are stored only for the locally owned block.\n- **Init**: every rank runs the identical deterministic `randomizeBodies`, so no broadcast is needed.\n- **Per step**: each rank computes forces for its own `i` range against all `j`, integrates its own positions, then three `MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, …)` calls (one per coordinate array, SoA layout) refresh the replicated positions.\n- **Single-rank speed**: the kernel was restructured to SoA and processes `VLEN = 8` bodies `i` at a time, each lane keeping its own accumulator over the full `j` loop. This gives SIMD without reassociating any summation, so per-body force sums keep the original order bit-for-bit. Position and velocity arrays are padded by `VLEN` so the partial trailing block uses the exact same kernel (surplus lanes computed and discarded) — no divergent scalar tail path.\n- **Output/validation**: velocities are gathered to rank 0, which prints results and runs `validateSimulation`; only rank 0 prints anything, so stdout matches the original.
